# Changed executable-line coverage

Coverage comes from stdlib `sys.settrace` during [B1_FINAL.log](/scratch/jjyeung/orch_n30/contracts_rebuild/out/B1_FINAL.log); the raw mapping is [B1_FINAL_trace.json](/scratch/jjyeung/orch_n30/contracts_rebuild/out/B1_FINAL_trace.json). `work/coverage_report.py` intersects added/replaced diff lines with recursive `dis.findlinestarts` results. This measures executable lines, including module definitions/imports and changed inherited test code; it does not claim branch coverage. A `[collection]` entry identifies the test module whose import executed that line.

| Patch | Executed / changed executable lines | Unexecuted | Source |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| geometry | 204/204 | 0 | [COVERAGE_DETAIL.json](/scratch/jjyeung/orch_n30/contracts_rebuild/out/COVERAGE_DETAIL.json) |
| candidates | 245/245 | 0 | [COVERAGE_DETAIL.json](/scratch/jjyeung/orch_n30/contracts_rebuild/out/COVERAGE_DETAIL.json) |
| box | 9/9 | 0 | [COVERAGE_DETAIL.json](/scratch/jjyeung/orch_n30/contracts_rebuild/out/COVERAGE_DETAIL.json) |
